plugins {
    id 'com.android.application'
}

android {
    namespace 'com.n3th3r.aoddimmer'
    compileSdk 34

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId "com.n3th3r.aoddimmer"
        minSdk 26
        targetSdk 34
        versionCode 1
        versionName "1.0"
    }

    buildTypes {
        release {
            minifyEnabled false
            proguardFiles getDefaultProguardFile('proguard-android-optimize.txt'), 'proguard-rules.pro'
        }
    }
    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility JavaVersion.VERSION_1_8
        targetCompatibility JavaVersion.VERSION_1_8
    }
}

dependencies {
    compileOnly 'de.robv.android.xposed:api:82'
    implementation 'androidx.appcompat:appcompat:1.6.1'
    implementation 'com.google.android.material:material:1.11.0'
    implementation 'androidx.preference:preference:1.2.1'
}

        android:allowBackup="true"
        android:icon="@android:drawable/ic_menu_view"

        android:roundIcon="@android:drawable/ic_menu_view"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@style/Theme.AodDimmer">

        <!-- LSPosed / Xposed Module Metadata -->
        <meta-data
            android:name="xposedmodule"
            android:value="true" />
        <meta-data
            android:name="xposeddescription"
            android:value="Adjust and enhance post-delay Always-On Display (AOD) dimming on MIUI &amp; HyperOS" />
        <meta-data
            android:name="xposedminversion"
            android:value="82" />

            android:name="xposedscope"

`AodDimmer/app/src/main/java/com/n3th3r/aoddimmer/MainActivity.java`:

```java
package com.n3th3r.aoddimmer;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.widget.SeekBar;
import android.widget.TextView;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;
import androidx.appcompat.widget.SwitchCompat;

public class MainActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private static final String PREF_NAME = "aod_dimmer_prefs";
    private SharedPreferences mPrefs;

    // Master
    private SwitchCompat mSwitchMaster;

    // Night Anti-Runaway
    private SwitchCompat mSwitchNightGuard;
    private SeekBar mSeekBarNightMaxCap;
    private TextView mTvNightMaxCap;
    private SeekBar mSeekBarNightLuxThreshold;
    private TextView mTvNightLuxThreshold;

    // Daytime Anti-Flicker
    private SwitchCompat mSwitchDaytimeAntiFlicker;
    private SeekBar mSeekBarDiffThreshold;
    private TextView mTvDiffThreshold;
    private SeekBar mSeekBarThrottle;
    private TextView mTvThrottle;
    private SwitchCompat mSwitchSmoothing;

    // AOD Dimmer
    private SwitchCompat mSwitchAodEnabled;
    private SeekBar mSeekBarMinNits;
    private TextView mTvMinNits;
    private SeekBar mSeekBarMaxNits;
    private TextView mTvMaxNits;

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_main);

        mPrefs = getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE);

        // Bind Views
        mSwitchMaster = findViewById(R.id.switch_enabled);

        mSwitchNightGuard = findViewById(R.id.switch_night_guard);
        mSeekBarNightMaxCap = findViewById(R.id.seekbar_night_max_cap);
        mTvNightMaxCap = findViewById(R.id.tv_night_max_cap);
        mSeekBarNightLuxThreshold = findViewById(R.id.seekbar_night_lux_threshold);
        mTvNightLuxThreshold = findViewById(R.id.tv_night_lux_threshold);

        mSwitchDaytimeAntiFlicker = findViewById(R.id.switch_daytime_anti_flicker);
        mSeekBarDiffThreshold = findViewById(R.id.seekbar_diff_threshold);
        mTvDiffThreshold = findViewById(R.id.tv_diff_threshold);
        mSeekBarThrottle = findViewById(R.id.seekbar_throttle);
        mTvThrottle = findViewById(R.id.tv_throttle_interval);
        mSwitchSmoothing = findViewById(R.id.switch_smoothing);

        mSwitchAodEnabled = findViewById(R.id.switch_aod_enabled);
        mSeekBarMinNits = findViewById(R.id.seekbar_min_nits);
        mTvMinNits = findViewById(R.id.tv_min_nits);
        mSeekBarMaxNits = findViewById(R.id.seekbar_max_nits);
        mTvMaxNits = findViewById(R.id.tv_max_nits);

        // Load values
        boolean masterEnabled = mPrefs.getBoolean("module_enabled", true);
        boolean nightGuardEnabled = mPrefs.getBoolean("night_anti_runaway_enabled", true);
        int nightMaxCap = mPrefs.getInt("night_max_brightness_percent", 25);
        int nightLuxThreshold = mPrefs.getInt("night_lux_threshold", 25);

        boolean daytimeAntiFlicker = mPrefs.getBoolean("daytime_anti_flicker_enabled", true);
        int diffPercent = mPrefs.getInt("sensor_diff_threshold_percent", 25);
        int throttleMs = mPrefs.getInt("sensor_throttle_ms", 2000);
        boolean smoothingEnabled = mPrefs.getBoolean("lux_smoothing_enabled", true);

        boolean aodEnabled = mPrefs.getBoolean("aod_dimmer_enabled", true);
        float minNits = mPrefs.getFloat("min_nits_val", 2.0f);
        int maxNits = mPrefs.getInt("max_nits_val", 25);

        // Populate UI
        mSwitchMaster.setChecked(masterEnabled);

        mSwitchNightGuard.setChecked(nightGuardEnabled);
        mSeekBarNightMaxCap.setProgress(Math.max(0, Math.min(40, nightMaxCap - 10)));
        mTvNightMaxCap.setText(nightMaxCap + "%");

        mSeekBarNightLuxThreshold.setProgress(Math.max(0, Math.min(55, nightLuxThreshold - 5)));
        mTvNightLuxThreshold.setText(nightLuxThreshold + " lux");

        mSwitchDaytimeAntiFlicker.setChecked(daytimeAntiFlicker);
        mSeekBarDiffThreshold.setProgress(Math.max(0, Math.min(40, diffPercent - 10)));
        mTvDiffThreshold.setText(diffPercent + "%");

        int throttleProgress = (throttleMs - 500) / 500;
        mSeekBarThrottle.setProgress(Math.max(0, Math.min(9, throttleProgress)));
        mTvThrottle.setText(String.format("%.1fs", throttleMs / 1000.0f));

        mSwitchSmoothing.setChecked(smoothingEnabled);

        mSwitchAodEnabled.setChecked(aodEnabled);
        int minProgress = Math.round((minNits - 0.5f) / 0.5f);
        mSeekBarMinNits.setProgress(Math.max(0, Math.min(19, minProgress)));
        mTvMinNits.setText(String.format("%.1f nits", minNits));

        mSeekBarMaxNits.setProgress(Math.max(0, Math.min(110, maxNits - 10)));
        mTvMaxNits.setText(maxNits + " nits");

        // Listeners
        mSwitchMaster.setOnCheckedChangeListener((btn, isChecked) -> {
            mPrefs.edit().putBoolean("module_enabled", isChecked).apply();
            makeWorldReadable();
            Toast.makeText(this, isChecked ? "Display Stabilizer Enabled" : "Display Stabilizer Disabled", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        });

        mSwitchNightGuard.setOnCheckedChangeListener((btn, isChecked) -> {
            mPrefs.edit().putBoolean("night_anti_runaway_enabled", isChecked).apply();
            makeWorldReadable();
        });

        mSeekBarNightMaxCap.setOnSeekBarChangeListener(new SimpleSeekBarListener() {
            @Override
            public void onProgressChanged(SeekBar seekBar, int progress, boolean fromUser) {
                int val = 10 + progress;
                mTvNightMaxCap.setText(val + "%");
                mPrefs.edit().putInt("night_max_brightness_percent", val).apply();
                makeWorldReadable();
            }
        });

        mSeekBarNightLuxThreshold.setOnSeekBarChangeListener(new SimpleSeekBarListener() {
            @Override
            public void onProgressChanged(SeekBar seekBar, int progress, boolean fromUser) {
                int val = 5 + progress;
                mTvNightLuxThreshold.setText(val + " lux");
                mPrefs.edit().putInt("night_lux_threshold", val).apply();
                makeWorldReadable();
            }
        });

        mSwitchDaytimeAntiFlicker.setOnCheckedChangeListener((btn, isChecked) -> {
            mPrefs.edit().putBoolean("daytime_anti_flicker_enabled", isChecked).apply();
            makeWorldReadable();
        });

        mSeekBarDiffThreshold.setOnSeekBarChangeListener(new SimpleSeekBarListener() {
            @Override
            public void onProgressChanged(SeekBar seekBar, int progress, boolean fromUser) {
                int val = 10 + progress;
                mTvDiffThreshold.setText(val + "%");
                mPrefs.edit().putInt("sensor_diff_threshold_percent", val).apply();
                makeWorldReadable();
            }
        });

        mSeekBarThrottle.setOnSeekBarChangeListener(new SimpleSeekBarListener() {
            @Override
            public void onProgressChanged(SeekBar seekBar, int progress, boolean fromUser) {
                int ms = 500 + progress * 500;
                mTvThrottle.setText(String.format("%.1fs", ms / 1000.0f));
                mPrefs.edit().putInt("sensor_throttle_ms", ms).apply();
                makeWorldReadable();
            }
        });

        mSwitchSmoothing.setOnCheckedChangeListener((btn, isChecked) -> {
            mPrefs.edit().putBoolean("lux_smoothing_enabled", isChecked).apply();
            makeWorldReadable();
        });

        mSwitchAodEnabled.setOnCheckedChangeListener((btn, isChecked) -> {
            mPrefs.edit().putBoolean("aod_dimmer_enabled", isChecked).apply();
            makeWorldReadable();
        });

        mSeekBarMinNits.setOnSeekBarChangeListener(new SimpleSeekBarListener() {
            @Override
            public void onProgressChanged(SeekBar seekBar, int progress, boolean fromUser) {
                float val = 0.5f + progress * 0.5f;
                mTvMinNits.setText(String.format("%.1f nits", val));
                mPrefs.edit().putFloat("min_nits_val", val).apply();
                makeWorldReadable();
            }
        });

        mSeekBarMaxNits.setOnSeekBarChangeListener(new SimpleSeekBarListener() {
            @Override
            public void onProgressChanged(SeekBar seekBar, int progress, boolean fromUser) {
                int val = 10 + progress;
                mTvMaxNits.setText(val + " nits");
                mPrefs.edit().putInt("max_nits_val", val).apply();
                makeWorldReadable();
            }
        });

        makeWorldReadable();
    }

    private void makeWorldReadable() {
        try {
            java.io.File prefFile = new java.io.File(
                getApplicationInfo().dataDir, 
                "shared_prefs/" + PREF_NAME + ".xml"
            );
            if (prefFile.exists()) {
                prefFile.setReadable(true, false);
                prefFile.setExecutable(true, false);
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    private static abstract class SimpleSeekBarListener implements SeekBar.OnSeekBarChangeListener {
        @Override
        public void onStartTrackingTouch(SeekBar seekBar) {}

        @Override
        public void onStopTrackingTouch(SeekBar seekBar) {}
    }
}

```

`AodDimmer/app/src/main/java/com/n3th3r/aoddimmer/MainHook.java`:

```java
package com.n3th3r.aoddimmer;

import de.robv.android.xposed.IXposedHookLoadPackage;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XSharedPreferences;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam;

public class MainHook implements IXposedHookLoadPackage {

    private static final String TAG = "[AodDimmer] ";
    private static final String PREF_NAME = "aod_dimmer_prefs";
    private static XSharedPreferences sPrefs;

    // Sensor debouncing and smoothing state
    private static long sLastSensorEventTime = 0;
    private static float sLastAcceptedLux = -1.0f;
    private static float sSmoothedLux = -1.0f;

    private static XSharedPreferences getPrefs() {
        if (sPrefs == null) {
            sPrefs = new XSharedPreferences("com.n3th3r.aoddimmer", PREF_NAME);
            sPrefs.makeWorldReadable();
        } else {
            sPrefs.reload();
        }
        return sPrefs;
    }

    @Override
    public void handleLoadPackage(LoadPackageParam lpparam) throws Throwable {
        if (!"android".equals(lpparam.packageName)) {
            return;
        }

        XposedBridge.log(TAG + "Initializing AOD & Auto-Brightness Stabilizer in system_server...");

        // 1. Hook AOSP AutomaticBrightnessController for Anti-Runaway and Anti-Flicker
        try {
            Class<?> abcClass = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.android.server.display.AutomaticBrightnessController",
                lpparam.classLoader
            );

            // A. Sensor Event Filtering: Debounce jitter, EMA smoothing, and upward rate-limiting
            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(
                abcClass,
                "handleLightSensorEvent",
                long.class,
                float.class,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XSharedPreferences prefs = getPrefs();
                        if (!prefs.getBoolean("module_enabled", true)) {
                            return;
                        }

                        long time = (long) param.args[0];
                        float rawLux = (float) param.args[1];

                        boolean antiFlicker = prefs.getBoolean("daytime_anti_flicker_enabled", true);
                        int throttleMs = prefs.getInt("sensor_throttle_ms", 2000);
                        int diffPercent = prefs.getInt("sensor_diff_threshold_percent", 25);
                        float diffThreshold = Math.max(0.05f, diffPercent / 100.0f);
                        boolean smoothing = prefs.getBoolean("lux_smoothing_enabled", true);

                        // Daytime Debouncing / Jitter Suppression
                        if (antiFlicker && sLastSensorEventTime > 0 && (time - sLastSensorEventTime) < throttleMs) {
                            if (sLastAcceptedLux >= 0) {
                                float diffRatio = Math.abs(rawLux - sLastAcceptedLux) / Math.max(1.0f, sLastAcceptedLux);
                                if (diffRatio < diffThreshold) {
                                    // Suppress redundant jitter
                                    param.setResult(null);
                                    return;
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Low-pass EMA filter for silky smooth transitions without flickering
                        float effectiveLux = rawLux;
                        if (smoothing) {
                            if (sSmoothedLux < 0) {
                                sSmoothedLux = rawLux;
                            } else {
                                // Slower upward ramp (0.20), faster downward ramp (0.40)
                                float alpha = (rawLux > sSmoothedLux) ? 0.20f : 0.40f;
                                sSmoothedLux = sSmoothedLux + alpha * (rawLux - sSmoothedLux);
                            }
                            effectiveLux = sSmoothedLux;
                        }

                        sLastSensorEventTime = time;
                        sLastAcceptedLux = effectiveLux;
                        param.args[1] = effectiveLux;
                    }
                }
            );

            // B. Anti-Runaway Protection: Cap max brightness when reading in dark environments
            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(
                abcClass,
                "updateAutoBrightness",
                boolean.class,
                boolean.class,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XSharedPreferences prefs = getPrefs();
                        if (!prefs.getBoolean("module_enabled", true)) {
                            return;
                        }

                        if (!prefs.getBoolean("night_anti_runaway_enabled", true)) {
                            return;
                        }

                        Object thisObj = param.thisObject;
                        float ambientLux = XposedHelpers.getFloatField(thisObj, "mAmbientLux");
                        int luxTrigger = prefs.getInt("night_lux_threshold", 25);

                        // If ambient light is in the dark/night zone (reading comic in dark)
                        if (ambientLux <= (float) luxTrigger) {
                            float maxCap = prefs.getInt("night_max_brightness_percent", 25) / 100.0f;
                            float currentBrightness = XposedHelpers.getFloatField(thisObj, "mScreenAutoBrightness");

                            if (currentBrightness > maxCap) {
                                XposedHelpers.setFloatField(thisObj, "mScreenAutoBrightness", maxCap);
                                try {
                                    XposedHelpers.setFloatField(thisObj, "mRawScreenAutoBrightness", maxCap);
                                } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

                                XposedBridge.log(TAG + "Night Runaway Guard active: clamped brightness "
                                    + currentBrightness + " -> " + maxCap + " (ambient=" + ambientLux + " lux)");
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            );

            XposedBridge.log(TAG + "Successfully hooked AutomaticBrightnessController for AOSP!");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedBridge.log(TAG + "Could not hook AutomaticBrightnessController: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // 2. Hook AOSP DozeBrightnessStrategy for Custom ROM AOD Dimming
        try {
            Class<?> dozeStrategyClass = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.android.server.display.brightness.strategy.DozeBrightnessStrategy",
                lpparam.classLoader
            );

            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(
                dozeStrategyClass,
                "updateBrightness",
                "com.android.server.display.brightness.StrategyExecutionRequest",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XSharedPreferences prefs = getPrefs();
                        if (!prefs.getBoolean("module_enabled", true) || !prefs.getBoolean("aod_dimmer_enabled", true)) {
                            return;
                        }

                        Object stateObj = param.getResult();
                        if (stateObj == null) {
                            return;
                        }

                        float currentBrightness = XposedHelpers.getFloatField(stateObj, "mBrightness");
                        if (Float.isNaN(currentBrightness) || currentBrightness <= 0f) {
                            return;
                        }

                        float userMinNits = prefs.getFloat("min_nits_val", 2.0f);
                        float userMaxNits = (float) prefs.getInt("max_nits_val", 25);

                        // Normalized fallback ratio: 2 nits ~ 0.015f, 25 nits ~ 0.08f
                        float minCap = Math.max(0.005f, userMinNits / 150.0f);
                        float maxCap = Math.max(minCap, userMaxNits / 120.0f);

                        float targetBrightness = Math.max(minCap, Math.min(maxCap, currentBrightness));
                        XposedHelpers.setFloatField(stateObj, "mBrightness", targetBrightness);

                        XposedBridge.log(TAG + "AOSP Doze Brightness shaped: " + currentBrightness + " -> " + targetBrightness);
                    }
                }
            );

            XposedBridge.log(TAG + "Successfully hooked DozeBrightnessStrategy for AOSP!");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedBridge.log(TAG + "Could not hook DozeBrightnessStrategy: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // 3. Fallback Hook: HyperOS DozeAutoBrightnessController (Dual-ROM compatibility)
        try {
            Class<?> hyperosDozeClass = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.android.server.display.DozeAutoBrightnessController",
                lpparam.classLoader
            );

            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(
                hyperosDozeClass,
                "handleLightSensorEvent",
                long.class,
                float.class,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XSharedPreferences prefs = getPrefs();
                        if (!prefs.getBoolean("module_enabled", true) || !prefs.getBoolean("aod_dimmer_enabled", true)) {
                            return;
                        }
                        long time = (long) param.args[0];
                        float lux = (float) param.args[1];
                        int throttleMs = prefs.getInt("sensor_throttle_ms", 2000);
                        if (sLastSensorEventTime > 0 && (time - sLastSensorEventTime) < throttleMs) {
                            param.setResult(null);
                        }
                    }
                }
            );

            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(
                hyperosDozeClass,
                "updateAutoBrightness",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XSharedPreferences prefs = getPrefs();
                        if (!prefs.getBoolean("module_enabled", true) || !prefs.getBoolean("aod_dimmer_enabled", true)) {
                            return;
                        }
                        Object thisObj = param.thisObject;
                        float userMinNits = prefs.getFloat("min_nits_val", 2.0f);
                        float userMaxNits = (float) prefs.getInt("max_nits_val", 25);
                        Object ddc = XposedHelpers.getObjectField(thisObj, "mDisplayDeviceConfig");
                        if (ddc != null) {
                            float minB = (float) XposedHelpers.callMethod(ddc, "getBrightnessFromNit", userMinNits);
                            float maxB = (float) XposedHelpers.callMethod(ddc, "getBrightnessFromNit", userMaxNits);
                            float cur = XposedHelpers.getFloatField(thisObj, "mCurrentBrightness");
                            float target = Math.max(minB, Math.min(maxB, cur));
                            XposedHelpers.setFloatField(thisObj, "mCurrentBrightness", target);
                        }
                    }
                }
            );
            XposedBridge.log(TAG + "Successfully hooked HyperOS DozeAutoBrightnessController fallback!");
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }
}

```

`IntuneBypass/build.ps1`:

```ps1
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$ProjectDir = $PSScriptRoot
$RootDir = Split-Path $ProjectDir -Parent
$BuildDir = "$ProjectDir\build"
$ClassesDir = "$BuildDir\classes"
$ResZip = "$BuildDir\res.zip"
$UnsignedApk = "$BuildDir\unsigned.apk"
$AlignedApk = "$BuildDir\aligned.apk"
$SignedApk = "$RootDir\IntuneBypass.apk"

$SdkDir = if ($env:LOCALAPPDATA) { "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\Android\Sdk" } else { "$HOME\AppData\Local\Android\Sdk" }
$BuildTools = "$SdkDir\build-tools\35.0.0"
$AndroidJar = "$SdkDir\platforms\android-36\android.jar"
$XposedJar = "$HOME\.gradle\caches\modules-2\files-2.1\de.robv.android.xposed\api\82\35866b507b360d4789ff389ad7386b6e8bbf6cc4\api-82.jar"
$JdkBin = "C:\Program Files\Microsoft\jdk-17.0.20.101-hotspot\bin"

if (Test-Path $BuildDir) {
    Remove-Item -Recurse -Force $BuildDir
}
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $ClassesDir | Out-Null

& "$BuildTools\aapt2.exe" compile --dir "$ProjectDir\res" -o $ResZip
& "$BuildTools\aapt2.exe" link -o $UnsignedApk `
    -I $AndroidJar `
    --manifest "$ProjectDir\AndroidManifest.xml" `
    --java "$BuildDir\gen" `
    -A "$ProjectDir\assets" `
    $ResZip

$JavaFiles = Get-ChildItem -Recurse -Path "$ProjectDir\src", "$BuildDir\gen" -Filter "*.java" | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName }
& "$JdkBin\javac.exe" --release 8 -cp "$AndroidJar;$XposedJar" -d $ClassesDir $JavaFiles

$ClassFiles = Get-ChildItem -Recurse -Path $ClassesDir -Filter "*.class" | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName }
& "$BuildTools\d8.bat" --lib $AndroidJar --classpath $XposedJar --output $BuildDir $ClassFiles

& "$JdkBin\jar.exe" -uf $UnsignedApk -C $BuildDir classes.dex

& "$BuildTools\zipalign.exe" -f -p 4 $UnsignedApk $AlignedApk

$Keystore = "$HOME\.android\debug.keystore"
if (-not (Test-Path $Keystore)) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $Keystore) | Out-Null
    & "$JdkBin\keytool.exe" -genkeypair -v -keystore $Keystore -storepass android -alias androiddebugkey -keypass android -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 -dname "CN=Android Debug,O=Android,C=US"
}

& "$BuildTools\apksigner.bat" sign --ks $Keystore --ks-pass pass:android --key-pass pass:android --out $SignedApk $AlignedApk
Write-Output "Built signed APK: $SignedApk"

```

`IntuneBypass/src/com/n3th3r/intunebypass/ClassLoaderHooker.java`:

```java
package com.n3th3r.intunebypass;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.ContextWrapper;
import dalvik.system.BaseDexClassLoader;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;

public final class ClassLoaderHooker {
    private static final AtomicBoolean GLOBAL_INSTALLED = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private static final Set<ClassLoader> HOOKED_LOADERS =
            Collections.newSetFromMap(new ConcurrentHashMap<ClassLoader, Boolean>());
    private static final Set<Class<?>> HOOKED_CLASSES =
            Collections.newSetFromMap(new ConcurrentHashMap<Class<?>, Boolean>());
    private static final ThreadLocal<Boolean> IN_INTERNAL_LOOKUP =
            new ThreadLocal<Boolean>() {
                @Override
                protected Boolean initialValue() {
                    return Boolean.FALSE;
                }
            };

    private ClassLoaderHooker() {}

    public static void installGlobalHooks(final String packageName) {
        if (!GLOBAL_INSTALLED.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            return;
        }
        hookDexClassLoaderConstructors(packageName);
        hookCreatePackageContext(packageName);
    }

    public static void hookClassLoader(ClassLoader cl, String packageName) {
        if (cl == null || isFrameworkHookLoader(cl) || !HOOKED_LOADERS.add(cl)) {
            return;
        }
        IN_INTERNAL_LOOKUP.set(Boolean.TRUE);
        try {
            IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking ClassLoader in " + packageName + ": " + cl.getClass().getName());
            MamBoundaryHooks.installAll(cl);
            ObfuscatedTargetHooks.installAll(cl);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            IntuneBypassInit.log("Error hooking ClassLoader: " + t.getMessage());
        } finally {
            IN_INTERNAL_LOOKUP.set(Boolean.FALSE);
        }
    }

    public static boolean markClassHooked(Class<?> cls) {
        return cls != null && HOOKED_CLASSES.add(cls);
    }

    private static boolean isFrameworkHookLoader(ClassLoader cl) {
        String name = cl.getClass().getName();
        return name.contains("InMemoryDexClassLoader")
                || name.contains("LSP")
                || name.contains("Vector");
    }

    private static void hookDexClassLoaderConstructors(final String packageName) {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllConstructors(BaseDexClassLoader.class, new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                    if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(IN_INTERNAL_LOOKUP.get())) {
                        return;
                    }
                    if (param.thisObject instanceof ClassLoader) {
                        hookClassLoader((ClassLoader) param.thisObject, packageName);
                    }
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void hookCreatePackageContext(final String packageName) {
        try {
            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(
                    ContextWrapper.class,
                    "createPackageContext",
                    String.class,
                    int.class,
                    new XC_MethodHook() {
                        @Override
                        protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                            if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(IN_INTERNAL_LOOKUP.get())) {
                                return;
                            }
                            Object res = param.getResult();
                            if (res instanceof Context) {
                                ClassLoader cl = ((Context) res).getClassLoader();
                                hookClassLoader(cl, packageName);
                            }
                        }
                    });
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }
}

```

`IntuneBypass/src/com/n3th3r/intunebypass/IntuneBypassInit.java`:

```java
package com.n3th3r.intunebypass;

import android.os.Build;
import de.robv.android.xposed.IXposedHookLoadPackage;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;
import java.io.File;

public final class IntuneBypassInit implements IXposedHookLoadPackage {
    private static final String TAG = "IntuneBypass";

    public static void log(String msg) {
        XposedBridge.log(TAG + ": " + msg);
    }

    @Override
    public void handleLoadPackage(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        if (lpparam == null || lpparam.packageName == null) {
            return;
        }
        sanitizeBuildTags();
        hookFileExists();
        ClassLoaderHooker.installGlobalHooks(lpparam.packageName);
        ClassLoaderHooker.hookClassLoader(lpparam.classLoader, lpparam.packageName);
    }

    private static void sanitizeBuildTags() {
        try {
            if (Build.TAGS != null && Build.TAGS.contains("test-keys")) {
                XposedHelpers.setStaticObjectField(Build.class, "TAGS", "release-keys");
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void hookFileExists() {
        try {
            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(File.class, "exists", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                    File file = (File) param.thisObject;
                    if (file == null) {
                        return;
                    }
                    String path = file.getPath();
                    if (isBlockedPath(path)) {
                        param.setResult(Boolean.FALSE);
                    }
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static boolean isBlockedPath(String path) {
        if (path == null) {
            return false;
        }
        String lower = path.toLowerCase();
        return lower.endsWith("/su")
                || lower.endsWith("/magisk")
                || lower.endsWith("/ksud")
                || lower.endsWith("/busybox")
                || lower.contains("superuser.apk")
                || lower.contains("xposedbridge.jar")
                || lower.contains("lsposed");
    }
}

```

`IntuneBypass/src/com/n3th3r/intunebypass/MamBoundaryHooks.java`:

```java
package com.n3th3r.intunebypass;

import android.database.MatrixCursor;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodReplacement;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import java.lang.reflect.Constructor;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.Queue;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;

public final class MamBoundaryHooks {
    private static final String[] BOUNDARY_CLASSES = {
        "com.microsoft.intune.origin.InstrumentationCheck",
        "com.microsoft.intune.origin.abstraction.DeviceOriginContentProvider",
        "com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.ipc.AbstractAppPolicyEndpoint",
        "com.microsoft.omadm.apppolicy.MDMAppPolicyEndpoint",
        "com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.ipc.AppPolicyEndpointProxy",
        "com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.ipc.AppPolicyEndpointWrapper",
        "com.microsoft.intune.mam.policy.PolicyChecker",
        "com.microsoft.intune.mam.policy.security.ComplianceVerdictManagerImpl",
        "com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.app.startup.ConditionalLaunchGetAccessFragment"
    };

    private MamBoundaryHooks() {}

    public static void installAll(ClassLoader cl) {
        for (String name : BOUNDARY_CLASSES) {
            Class<?> cls = XposedHelpers.findClassIfExists(name, cl);
            if (cls != null && ClassLoaderHooker.markClassHooked(cls)) {
                hookLoadedClass(cls);
            }
        }
    }

    public static void hookLoadedClass(Class<?> cls) {
        String name = cls.getName();
        if ("com.microsoft.intune.origin.InstrumentationCheck".equals(name)) {
            hookInstrumentationCheck(cls);
        } else if ("com.microsoft.intune.origin.abstraction.DeviceOriginContentProvider".equals(name)) {
            hookDeviceOriginContentProvider(cls);
        } else if (name.endsWith("AppPolicyEndpoint")
                || name.endsWith("AppPolicyEndpointProxy")
                || name.endsWith("AppPolicyEndpointWrapper")) {
            hookAppPolicyEndpoint(cls);
        } else if ("com.microsoft.intune.mam.policy.PolicyChecker".equals(name)) {
            hookPolicyChecker(cls);
        } else if ("com.microsoft.intune.mam.policy.security.ComplianceVerdictManagerImpl".equals(name)) {
            hookComplianceVerdictManager(cls);
        } else if ("com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.app.startup.ConditionalLaunchGetAccessFragment".equals(name)) {
            hookConditionalLaunchFragment(cls);
        }
    }

    private static void hookInstrumentationCheck(Class<?> cls) {
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking InstrumentationCheck");
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            Class<?> ret = m.getReturnType();
            if (ret == int.class || ret == Integer.class) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, XC_MethodReplacement.returnConstant(0));
            } else if (ret == boolean.class || ret == Boolean.class) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, XC_MethodReplacement.returnConstant(Boolean.FALSE));
            }
        }
    }

    private static void hookDeviceOriginContentProvider(Class<?> cls) {
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking DeviceOriginContentProvider");
        XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(cls, "query", new XC_MethodReplacement() {
            @Override
            protected Object replaceHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                MatrixCursor cursor = new MatrixCursor(new String[] {"name", "value"}, 1);
                cursor.addRow(new Object[] {"origin", 0});
                return cursor;
            }
        });
        for (Field f : cls.getDeclaredFields()) {
            Class<?> fType = f.getType();
            if (fType.getName().startsWith("o.")) {
                ObfuscatedTargetHooks.hookOriginAggregatorClass(fType);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void hookAppPolicyEndpoint(final Class<?> cls) {
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking Endpoint: " + cls.getName());
        hookMethodReturnConstant(cls, "isDeviceCompliant", Boolean.TRUE);
        hookMethodReturnConstant(cls, "isXposeDetected", Boolean.FALSE);
        hookMethodReturnConstant(cls, "assessComplianceState", Boolean.TRUE);
        hookMethodReturnConstant(cls, "setRaspDetected", null);
        hookDestroySecrets(cls);
        hookAttestationResult(cls);
        discoverRaspManagerFromEndpoint(cls);
    }

    private static void hookMethodReturnConstant(Class<?> cls, String methodName, Object constant) {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(cls, methodName, XC_MethodReplacement.returnConstant(constant));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void hookDestroySecrets(Class<?> cls) {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(cls, "destroySecretsIfNecessary", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                    if (param.args.length >= 2 && param.args[1] != null) {
                        String reason = param.args[1].toString();
                        if (reason.contains("NON_COMPLIANT") || reason.contains("ATTESTATION")) {
                            IntuneBypassInit.log("Blocked destroySecretsIfNecessary for " + reason);
                            param.setResult(null);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void hookAttestationResult(final Class<?> cls) {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(cls, "getAttestationResult", new XC_MethodReplacement() {
                @Override
                protected Object replaceHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                    return createCompliantAttestationInfo(cls.getClassLoader());
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static Object createCompliantAttestationInfo(ClassLoader cl) {
        try {
            Class<?> infoCls = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                    "com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.ipc.DeviceAttestationInfo", cl);
            Class<? extends Enum> resEnum = (Class<? extends Enum>) XposedHelpers.findClass(
                    "com.microsoft.intune.mam.policy.DeviceAttestationAgentResult", cl);
            Class<? extends Enum> evalEnum = (Class<? extends Enum>) XposedHelpers.findClass(
                    "com.microsoft.intune.mam.policy.DeviceAttestationEvaluationType", cl);
            Object resVal = Enum.valueOf(resEnum, "DEVICE_INTEGRITY_AND_CTS");
            Object evalVal = Enum.valueOf(evalEnum, "HARDWARE_BACKED");
            Constructor<?> ctor = infoCls.getDeclaredConstructor(resEnum, evalEnum);
            ctor.setAccessible(true);
            return ctor.newInstance(resVal, evalVal);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            IntuneBypassInit.log("Failed to build DeviceAttestationInfo: " + t.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static void discoverRaspManagerFromEndpoint(Class<?> cls) {
        try {
            Field raspField = cls.getDeclaredField("mRaspManager");
            Class<?> raspCls = raspField.getType();
            if (raspCls != null && raspCls.getName().startsWith("o.")) {
                ObfuscatedTargetHooks.hookRaspManagerClass(raspCls);
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void hookPolicyChecker(Class<?> cls) {
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking PolicyChecker");
        hookMethodReturnConstant(cls, "onTransact", Boolean.TRUE);
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            String retName = m.getReturnType().getName();
            if (retName.endsWith("ConditionalLaunchAction")) {
                hookConditionalLaunchActionMethod(m);
            } else if (Queue.class.isAssignableFrom(m.getReturnType())) {
                hookQueueMethod(m);
            }
        }
        XposedBridge.hookAllConstructors(cls, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                if (param.args == null) {
                    return;
                }
                for (Object arg : param.args) {
                    if (arg != null && isOriginCheckManagerCandidate(arg.getClass())) {
                        ObfuscatedTargetHooks.hookOriginCheckManagerClass(arg.getClass());
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private static boolean isOriginCheckManagerCandidate(Class<?> candidate) {
        if (!candidate.getName().startsWith("o.")) {
            return false;
        }
        for (Method m : candidate.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            Class<?>[] params = m.getParameterTypes();
            if (m.getReturnType() == Future.class && params.length == 1 && params[0] == boolean.class) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static void hookConditionalLaunchActionMethod(Method method) {
        XposedBridge.hookMethod(method, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                Object res = param.getResult();
                if (res instanceof Enum<?>) {
                    String name = ((Enum<?>) res).name();
                    if (isBlockedLaunchAction(name)) {
                        Class<? extends Enum> enumCls = (Class<? extends Enum>) res.getClass();
                        param.setResult(Enum.valueOf(enumCls, "NONE"));
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private static void hookQueueMethod(Method method) {
        XposedBridge.hookMethod(method, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                Object res = param.getResult();
                if (res instanceof Queue<?>) {
                    Queue<?> queue = (Queue<?>) res;
                    Iterator<?> it = queue.iterator();
                    while (it.hasNext()) {
                        Object item = it.next();
                        if (item != null && isBlockedLaunchAction(item.toString())) {
                            it.remove();
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private static boolean isBlockedLaunchAction(String name) {
        return "DEVICE_NON_COMPLIANCE".equals(name)
                || "DEVICE_ATTESTATION_ERROR".equals(name)
                || "DEVICE_ATTESTATION_PROCESSING".equals(name)
                || "ORIGIN_CHECK_PENDING".equals(name);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static void hookComplianceVerdictManager(Class<?> cls) {
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking ComplianceVerdictManagerImpl");
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            if ("getComplianceResult".equals(m.getName()) && m.getReturnType().isEnum()) {
                final Class<? extends Enum> enumCls = (Class<? extends Enum>) m.getReturnType();
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodReplacement() {
                    @Override
                    protected Object replaceHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                        return getCompliantEnumValue(enumCls);
                    }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static void hookConditionalLaunchFragment(final Class<?> cls) {
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking ConditionalLaunchGetAccessFragment");
        hookMethodReturnConstant(cls, "areAllPolicyBucketsCompliant", Boolean.TRUE);
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            if ("getDeviceHealthyPoliciesResult".equals(m.getName()) && m.getReturnType().isEnum()) {
                final Class<? extends Enum> enumCls = (Class<? extends Enum>) m.getReturnType();
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodReplacement() {
                    @Override
                    protected Object replaceHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                        setDeviceHealthySuccess(param.thisObject, cls.getClassLoader());
                        return getCompliantEnumValue(enumCls);
                    }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static void setDeviceHealthySuccess(Object fragment, ClassLoader cl) {
        if (fragment == null) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            Class<? extends Enum> statusCls = (Class<? extends Enum>) XposedHelpers.findClass(
                    "com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.app.startup.ConditionalLaunchGetAccessFragment$ComplianceStatus",
                    cl);
            Object successVal = Enum.valueOf(statusCls, "COMPLIANCE_SUCCESS");
            XposedHelpers.setObjectField(fragment, "mDeviceHealthyCompliance", successVal);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static Object getCompliantEnumValue(Class<? extends Enum> enumCls) {
        try {
            return Enum.valueOf(enumCls, "COMPLIANT");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
            return Enum.valueOf(enumCls, "NONE");
        }
    }
}

```

`IntuneBypass/src/com/n3th3r/intunebypass/ObfuscatedTargetHooks.java`:

```java
package com.n3th3r.intunebypass;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodReplacement;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import java.lang.reflect.Constructor;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;

public final class ObfuscatedTargetHooks {
    private static final String[] OBFUSCATED_CLASSES = {
        "o.safeValue",
        "o.FirebaseNetworkException",
        "o.UiExecutor",
        "o.putSync",
        "o.accessgetEditLockp",
        "o.TransportBackend",
        "o.JsonIOException",
        "o.pushTrace"
    };
    private static final Set<Class<?>> HOOKED_DYNAMIC =
            Collections.newSetFromMap(new ConcurrentHashMap<Class<?>, Boolean>());

    private ObfuscatedTargetHooks() {}

    public static void installAll(ClassLoader cl) {
        for (String name : OBFUSCATED_CLASSES) {
            Class<?> cls = XposedHelpers.findClassIfExists(name, cl);
            if (cls != null && ClassLoaderHooker.markClassHooked(cls)) {
                hookLoadedClass(cls);
            }
        }
    }

    public static void hookLoadedClass(Class<?> cls) {
        String name = cls.getName();
        if ("o.safeValue".equals(name)) {
            hookOriginCheckManagerClass(cls);
        } else if ("o.FirebaseNetworkException".equals(name)) {
            hookRaspManagerClass(cls);
        } else if ("o.UiExecutor".equals(name)) {
            hookUiExecutor(cls);
        } else if ("o.putSync".equals(name)) {
            hookPutSync(cls);
        } else if ("o.accessgetEditLockp".equals(name)) {
            hookOriginAggregatorClass(cls);
        } else if ("o.TransportBackend".equals(name) || "o.JsonIOException".equals(name)) {
            hookXposedDetectorClass(cls);
        } else if ("o.pushTrace".equals(name)) {
            hookPushTrace(cls);
        }
    }

    public static void hookOriginCheckManagerClass(final Class<?> cls) {
        if (!HOOKED_DYNAMIC.add(cls)) {
            return;
        }
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking OriginCheckManager: " + cls.getName());
        XposedBridge.hookAllConstructors(cls, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                setBooleanFieldSafe(param.thisObject, "getU1", true);
            }
        });
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "UserChoiceDialog2", Boolean.TRUE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "onReceive", Boolean.TRUE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "MediaBrowserCompat", Boolean.TRUE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "read", Boolean.FALSE);
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            Class<?>[] params = m.getParameterTypes();
            if (m.getReturnType() == Future.class && params.length == 1 && params[0] == boolean.class) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodReplacement() {
                    @Override
                    protected Object replaceHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                        setBooleanFieldSafe(param.thisObject, "getU1", true);
                        return createReadyFutureTrue(cls.getClassLoader());
                    }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    public static void hookRaspManagerClass(Class<?> cls) {
        if (!HOOKED_DYNAMIC.add(cls)) {
            return;
        }
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking RaspManager: " + cls.getName());
        XposedBridge.hookAllConstructors(cls, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                setBooleanFieldSafe(param.thisObject, "ElephantEngine1", false);
            }
        });
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "MediaBrowserCompatMediaItem1", Boolean.FALSE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "INotificationSideChannelDefault", null);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "MediaBrowserCompatMediaBrowserImplApi23", null);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "sendEvent", null);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "setSelector", null);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "YubiKitUsbSmartcardCertBasedAuthManager11", null);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "YubiKitUsbSmartcardCertBasedAuthManager2", null);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "YubiKitUsbSmartcardCertBasedAuthManager3", null);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "MediaBrowserCompatMediaBrowserImplApi213", null);
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            if (m.getReturnType() == boolean.class && m.getParameterTypes().length == 1) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodReplacement() {
                    @Override
                    protected Object replaceHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                        setBooleanFieldSafe(param.thisObject, "ElephantEngine1", false);
                        return Boolean.FALSE;
                    }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    public static void hookOriginAggregatorClass(Class<?> cls) {
        if (!HOOKED_DYNAMIC.add(cls)) {
            return;
        }
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking OriginAggregator: " + cls.getName());
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            if (m.getReturnType() == int.class && m.getParameterTypes().length == 0) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, XC_MethodReplacement.returnConstant(0));
            }
        }
    }

    private static void hookXposedDetectorClass(Class<?> cls) {
        if (!HOOKED_DYNAMIC.add(cls)) {
            return;
        }
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking XposedDetector: " + cls.getName());
        for (Method m : cls.getDeclaredMethods()) {
            if (m.getReturnType() == int.class) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, XC_MethodReplacement.returnConstant(0));
            }
        }
    }

    private static void hookUiExecutor(Class<?> cls) {
        if (!HOOKED_DYNAMIC.add(cls)) {
            return;
        }
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking UiExecutor");
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "createIdToken", Boolean.FALSE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "asInterface", Boolean.FALSE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "INotificationSideChannelStub", 0);
    }

    private static void hookPutSync(Class<?> cls) {
        if (!HOOKED_DYNAMIC.add(cls)) {
            return;
        }
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking putSync (OriginDetectionResult)");
        XposedBridge.hookAllConstructors(cls, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) {
                if (param.args != null && param.args.length >= 1 && param.args[0] instanceof Integer) {
                    param.args[0] = 0;
                    for (int i = 1; i < param.args.length; i++) {
                        if (param.args[i] instanceof Long) {
                            param.args[i] = null;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private static void hookPushTrace(Class<?> cls) {
        if (!HOOKED_DYNAMIC.add(cls)) {
            return;
        }
        IntuneBypassInit.log("Hooking pushTrace (DeviceOriginUseCase)");
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "getInflatedId", Boolean.TRUE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "SignInIntrospectApiResultDefaultImpls", Boolean.TRUE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "SignInIntrospectApiResultRedirect", Boolean.FALSE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "SignInInitiateApiResultUserNotFound", Boolean.FALSE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "getBitmap", Boolean.FALSE);
        hookNamedMethodConstant(cls, "containsKey", null);
    }

    private static void hookNamedMethodConstant(Class<?> cls, String name, Object constant) {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(cls, name, XC_MethodReplacement.returnConstant(constant));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void setBooleanFieldSafe(Object obj, String fieldName, boolean value) {
        if (obj == null) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            XposedHelpers.setBooleanField(obj, fieldName, value);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static Object createReadyFutureTrue(ClassLoader cl) {
        try {
            Class<?> rfCls = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                    "com.microsoft.intune.mam.client.util.ReadyFuture", cl);
            Constructor<?> ctor = rfCls.getDeclaredConstructor(Object.class);
            ctor.setAccessible(true);
            return ctor.newInstance(Boolean.TRUE);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

```

# Android System Modifications & Tweaks Suite

A collection of systemless modifications, KernelSU / Magisk modules, and LSPosed hooks tailored for Xiaomi devices (HyperOS / MIUI) and AOSP Custom ROMs (including Project Infinity-X, Axion, Evolution X, and LineageOS).

## Mod Modules Overview

| Component | Target System | Type | Primary Purpose |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |

## Component 1: `IntuneBypass.apk` (Microsoft Intune & MAM Compliance Bypass)

### What It Does
Enables Microsoft Intune Company Portal and Intune-protected work apps (Microsoft Teams, Microsoft Outlook, Microsoft Authenticator, Edge, OneDrive, Office) to pass App Protection Policy (MAM) and Device Health compliance checks on rooted Android devices **without touching or modifying the target APKs**.

### How It Works Internally
- **Multi-ClassLoader Dynamic Hooking:** Intercepts `BaseDexClassLoader` construction and `ContextWrapper.createPackageContext` so hooks attach automatically to both the host application's secondary dynamic DEX loaders and the remote `com.microsoft.windowsintune.companyportal` MAM plugin context—without disturbing `@CallerSensitive` `Class.forName` stack frames.
- **MAM IPC & Policy Endpoint Overrides:** Hooks `DeviceOriginContentProvider.call` (`Origin = 0`), `InstrumentationCheck.isStepDebugged`, `AbstractAppPolicyEndpoint`, `MDMAppPolicyEndpoint`, `AppPolicyEndpointProxy`, and `AppPolicyEndpointWrapper` with exact Dalvik branch polarities (`checkOrigin = false`, `getDeviceOrigin = 0`, `shouldPerformInHouseOriginCheck = false`, `shouldPerformSafetyNetCheck = false`, `getIsDeviceRooted = false`, `isDeviceRooted = false`, `getOfflineRootDefenseLevel = 0`).
- **Compliance Verdict & UI Gate:** Overrides `PolicyChecker`, `ComplianceVerdictManagerImpl` (`ComplianceVerdict.COMPLIANT`), and `ConditionalLaunchGetAccessFragment` (`ConditionalLaunchAction.NONE` + `ComplianceStatus.COMPLIANCE_SUCCESS` + `areAllPolicyBucketsCompliant = true`).
- **DexGuard RASP & Xposed Detector Neutralization:** Neutralizes in-house origin bitmask aggregators (`o.accessgetEditLockp`, `o.safeValue`, `o.UiExecutor`, `o.putSync`, `o.pushTrace`), Xposed stack/class detectors (`o.TransportBackend`, `o.JsonIOException`), and DexGuard RASP callbacks (`o.FirebaseNetworkException`).

### What You Need (Prerequisites)
- **Root Manager:** KernelSU / KernelSU Next, Magisk, or APatch (keep **Unmount modules** enabled for target Microsoft apps in KernelSU Manager).
- **Xposed Framework:** **Vector (LSPosed)** or **LSPosed** (Zygisk).
- **HMA (Hide My Applist) is NOT required:** Because `IntuneBypass.apk` intercepts the origin checks, RASP callbacks, and policy verdicts directly in memory, you do not need HMA for Teams, Outlook, or Company Portal.
- **Play Integrity (Optional but Recommended):** Keep `PlayIntegrity-TrickyStore-AIO.zip` (or `TrickyStore-StrongIntegrity.zip`) active if your organization also requires full MDM Device Enrollment via Google Play Services.

### Installation & Scope Configuration

#### Method A: Via LSPosed / Vector Manager UI
1. Download and install **`IntuneBypass.apk`**.
2. Open **Vector / LSPosed Manager** -> **Modules** -> enable **Intune & MAM Compliance Bypass**.
3. Check the following apps in the module scope:
   - **Intune Company Portal** (`com.microsoft.windowsintune.companyportal`) — **Required** (hosts the MAM policy engine)
   - **Microsoft Teams** (`com.microsoft.teams`)
   - **Microsoft Outlook** (`com.microsoft.office.outlook`)
   - **Microsoft Authenticator** (`com.azure.authenticator`)
   - *(Plus any other Intune-protected Microsoft work app you use)*
4. Force-stop **Company Portal** and **Teams** (do **not** clear app data), then open **Teams**.
5. On the **Get Access** screen, tap **CONTINUE** to proceed directly into your workspace.

#### Method B: Via ADB / Root CLI (Vector v2+)
```bash
# Install module
adb install -r IntuneBypass.apk

# Enable module and configure scope via Vector CLI
adb shell "su -c '/data/adb/lspd/cli modules enable com.n3th3r.intunebypass && /data/adb/lspd/cli scope set com.n3th3r.intunebypass com.microsoft.windowsintune.companyportal/0 com.microsoft.teams/0 com.microsoft.office.outlook/0 com.azure.authenticator/0 && am force-stop com.microsoft.windowsintune.companyportal && am force-stop com.microsoft.teams'"
```

## Component 2: `PlayIntegrity-TrickyStore-AIO.zip` (All-in-One Play Integrity Suite)

### The Problem on Unlocked Bootloaders & Stock/Clean ROMs
On modern Android (especially HyperOS or clean AOSP without native spoofing), an unlocked bootloader causes Google Play Integrity to fail:
1. `MEETS_BASIC_INTEGRITY`: Fails due to exposed bootloader unlock flags.
2. `MEETS_DEVICE_INTEGRITY`: Fails because the ROM build fingerprint is uncertified or custom.
3. `MEETS_STRONG_INTEGRITY`: Fails because the hardware TEE / Keymint attestation reports an unlocked bootloader.

### How It Works Internally
This unified module combines two independent engines into a single flashable package:
- **Play Integrity Fork (v18):** Runs via Zygisk to hook `com.google.android.gms.unstable`, injecting certified build properties and masking sensitive bootloader properties (`ro.boot.verifiedbootstate green`, `ro.boot.flash.locked 1`, `ro.secureboot.lockstate locked`).
- **Tricky Store Engine (v1.4.1):** Operates via a userspace daemon injecting `libtricky_store.so` into the `keystore2` and `keymint` services to sign hardware attestation requests with a valid Google-certified attestation keybox.
- **Embedded Strong Keybox:** Pre-loaded with a verified Keybox certificate chain (`t.me/keyboxstrong @evokerr`), pre-configured `target.txt` (`com.google.android.gms`, `com.android.vending`), and auto-detected security patch level.

### Installation
1. Flash **`PlayIntegrity-TrickyStore-AIO.zip`** in KernelSU Next, Magisk, or APatch.
2. Reboot your device.
3. Verify all three integrity levels pass in Play Integrity Checker or TB Checker.

---

## Component 3: `TrickyStore-StrongIntegrity.zip` (Plug & Play Keystore Spoofer)

### Target Use Case
Feature-rich custom ROMs (such as Project Infinity-X, Evolution X, and CrDroid) already have Play Integrity Fork / property spoofing built into the system framework to pass Basic and Device integrity. Flashing a second Zygisk spoofer is redundant.

### What It Does
- Bundles official Tricky Store v1.4.1 with the verified Strong Integrity Keybox.
- The installer automatically creates `/data/adb/tricky_store/`, writes `keybox.xml`, configures `target.txt`, sets correct `0644` permissions, and auto-detects `ro.build.version.security_patch` to write `security_patch.txt`.
- Syncs a backup to `/sdcard/InfinityResources/keybox.xml`.
- Zero manual file management required across clean ROM flashes.

---

## Component 4: `AodDimmer.apk` (AOD Dimmer & Display Auto-Brightness Stabilizer)

### Key Features
- **AOSP 14–17 Display Auto-Brightness Stabilizer:**
  - **Daytime Anti-Flicker Smoothing:** Implements Exponential Moving Average (EMA) smoothing and upward rate-limiting on `AutomaticBrightnessController.handleLightSensorEvent` to eliminate sudden, distracting brightness jumps caused by shadows or sensor jitter.
  - **Night White-Background Anti-Runaway Cap:** When reading in bed or dark environments (ambient lux <= threshold), bright white backgrounds (e.g., web pages, comics, documents) reflect light back into the ambient light sensor, causing an escalating feedback loop that pushes brightness to maximum. The module detects dark ambient conditions and clamps maximum screen brightness to safe, comfortable nits.
- **Dual-Curve Always-On Display (AOD) Dimming:**
  - **Min Dim Brightness (Dark Room):** Set low-light level (*Default: 2.0 nits, Range: 0.5 – 10.0 nits*).
  - **Max Ambient Brightness (Lit Room):** Cap lit-room AOD brightness so the display won't jump to high nits (*Default: 30 nits, Range: 10 – 120 nits*).
  - **Outdoor Sunlight Scaling:** Scales smoothly under direct sun so notifications remain visible.
- **HyperOS & AOSP Architecture Support:**
  - AOSP 14–17 hooks into `com.android.server.display.AutomaticBrightnessController` and `DozeBrightnessStrategy` in `/system/framework/services.jar`.
  - HyperOS hooks into `com.android.server.display.DozeAutoBrightnessController` in `/system_ext/framework/miui-services.jar`.

### Installation
1. Install **`AodDimmer.apk`**.
2. In **LSPosed / Vector Manager**, enable the module and select the **System Framework (`android`)** scope.
3. Perform a **Soft Reboot** or full restart.
4. Launch the **AOD Dimmer** app to customize your brightness curves and anti-flicker parameters.

---

## Component 5: `aosp-no-keyboard-chin.zip` (AOSP Keyboard Chin & Down Arrow Remover)

### What It Does
Eliminates the dead navigation space and down-arrow spacer beneath soft keyboards on AOSP and custom ROMs with gesture navigation enabled.

### Internal Mechanism
Toggles `Settings.Secure.navbar_ime_space = 0`. The flashable module includes a boot-time service (`service.sh`) and an action button (`action.sh`) in KernelSU Manager.

### Quick ADB Method
```bash
# Disable keyboard chin & hide arrow
adb shell settings put secure navbar_ime_space 0

# Restore default chin & arrow
adb shell settings put secure navbar_ime_space 1
```

---

## Component 6: `hyperos-aon-aod.zip` (HyperOS AOD Feature-Gate Unlocker)

### What It Does
Systemlessly removes Xiaomi's 10-second AOD restriction on AMOLED devices running HyperOS 1.0–3.0 / MIUI 14.

### Internal Mechanism
Patches `/product/etc/device_features/<device_codename>.xml` systemlessly to set `is_only_support_keycode_goto=false`. Compatible with read-only EROFS partitions when paired with MetaModule.

---

- **Verified Device:** POCO X6 5G / Redmi Note 13 Pro 5G (`garnet` / `garnetp_in`)
- **Display Types:** AMOLED / OLED displays across Xiaomi, POCO, Redmi, Pixel, and OnePlus devices.
- **Supported Platforms:**
  - AOSP 14 / 15 / 16 / 17 (Project Infinity-X, Axion, LineageOS, Evolution X, PixelOS)
  - Xiaomi HyperOS 1.0 / 2.0 / 3.0
  - MIUI 14

`aosp-no-keyboard-chin/action.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# KernelSU action script to toggle keyboard chin on/off

CURRENT="$(settings get secure navbar_ime_space)"

if [ "$CURRENT" = "0" ]; then
    settings put secure navbar_ime_space 1
    echo "Keyboard chin restored (navbar_ime_space=1)"
else
    settings put secure navbar_ime_space 0
    echo "Keyboard chin removed (navbar_ime_space=0)"
fi

```

```md
# Magisk & KernelSU Modules

Pre-configured, zero-setup flashable modules for passing Google Play Integrity and Hardware Keystore Attestation.

## 1. TrickyStore-StrongIntegrity (`tricky-store-aio`)
- **File:** `TrickyStore-StrongIntegrity.zip`
- **Purpose:** Standalone Tricky Store v1.4.1 pre-configured with the verified Strong Integrity Keybox.
- **Features:**
  - Pre-bundles working ECDSA/RSA keybox certificate chain.
  - Automatically deploys `/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml` with `0644` permissions.
  - Automatically configures `/data/adb/tricky_store/target.txt` (`com.google.android.gms`, `com.android.vending`).
  - Auto-detects device security patch date (`ro.build.version.security_patch`) and generates `security_patch.txt`.
  - Syncs backup to `/sdcard/InfinityResources/keybox.xml`.
- **Best For:** Custom ROMs (Infinity-X, Evolution X, CrDroid, Matrixx) that already feature built-in Play Integrity Fork prop spoofing.

## 2. PlayIntegrity-TrickyStore-AIO (`playintegrity-trickystore-aio`)
- **File:** `PlayIntegrity-TrickyStore-AIO.zip`
- **Purpose:** Complete all-in-one integrity suite combining **Play Integrity Fork v18** and **Tricky Store v1.4.1**.
- **Features:**
  - Passes all 3 tiers: `MEETS_BASIC_INTEGRITY`, `MEETS_DEVICE_INTEGRITY`, and `MEETS_STRONG_INTEGRITY`.
  - Integrates Zygisk property spoofer for Google Play Services.
  - Hides bootloader unlock state and sensitive flags (`ro.boot.verifiedbootstate green`, `ro.secureboot.lockstate locked`).
  - Runs Tricky Store keystore injector daemon in parallel.
  - Completely self-installing: flash once in KernelSU / Magisk / APatch and reboot.
- **Best For:** HyperOS, MIUI, and clean AOSP / LineageOS ROMs without built-in fingerprint spoofing.

`modules/playintegrity-trickystore-aio/META-INF/com/google/android/update-binary`:

```
#!/sbin/sh

#################
# Initialization
#################

umask 022

# echo before loading util_functions
ui_print() { echo "$1"; }

require_new_magisk() {
  ui_print "*******************************"
  ui_print " Please install Magisk v20.4+! "
  ui_print "*******************************"
  exit 1
}

#########################
# Load util_functions.sh
#########################

OUTFD=$2
ZIPFILE=$3

mount /data 2>/dev/null

[ -f /data/adb/magisk/util_functions.sh ] || require_new_magisk
. /data/adb/magisk/util_functions.sh
[ $MAGISK_VER_CODE -lt 20400 ] && require_new_magisk

install_module
exit 0

`modules/playintegrity-trickystore-aio/autopif4.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh

if [ "$USER" != "root" -a "$(whoami 2>/dev/null)" != "root" ]; then
  echo "autopif4: need root permissions"; exit 1;
fi;
case "$HOME" in
  *termux*) echo "autopif4: need su root environment"; exit 1;;
esac;

until [ -z "$1" ]; do
  case "$1" in
    -h|--help|help) echo "sh autopif4.sh [-a|-s] [-m]"; exit 0;;
    -a|--advanced|advanced) ARGS="-a"; shift;;
    -s|--strong|strong) FORCE_STRONG=1; shift;;
    -m|--match|match) FORCE_MATCH=1; shift;;
    *) break;;
  esac;
done;

echo "Pixel Canary pif.prop generator script \
  \n  by osm0sis @ xda-developers";

case "$0" in
  *.sh) DIR="$0";;
  *) DIR="$(lsof -p $$ 2>/dev/null | grep -o '/.*autopif4.sh$')";;
esac;
DIR=$(dirname "$(readlink -f "$DIR")");

item() { echo "\n- $@"; }
die() { echo "\nError: $@!"; exit 1; }
die_bb() { die "$@, install busybox"; }
warn() { echo "\nWarning: $@!"; }

find_busybox() {
  [ -n "$BUSYBOX" ] && return 0;
  local path;
  for path in /data/adb/modules/busybox-ndk/system/*/busybox /data/adb/magisk/busybox /data/adb/ksu/bin/busybox /data/adb/ap/bin/busybox; do
    if [ -f "$path" ]; then
      BUSYBOX="$path";
      return 0;
    fi;
  done;
  return 1;
}

if ! which wget >/dev/null || grep -q "wget-curl" $(which wget); then
  if ! find_busybox; then
    die_bb "wget not found";
  elif $BUSYBOX ping -c1 -s2 android.com 2>&1 | grep -q "bad address"; then
    die_bb "wget broken";
  else
    wget() { $BUSYBOX wget "$@"; }
  fi;
fi;

if date -D '%s' -d "$(date '+%s')" 2>&1 | grep -qE "bad date|invalid option"; then
  if ! find_busybox; then
    die_bb "date broken";
  else
    date() { $BUSYBOX date "$@"; }
  fi;
fi;

if ! echo "A\nB" | grep -m1 -A1 "A" | grep -q "B"; then
  if ! find_busybox; then
    die_bb "grep broken";
  else
    grep() { $BUSYBOX grep "$@"; }
  fi;
fi;

if [ "$DIR" = /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix ]; then
  DIR=$DIR/autopif4;
  mkdir -p $DIR;
fi;
cd "$DIR";

item "Crawling Android Developers for latest Pixel Beta device list ...";
wget -q -T 10 -O PIXEL_VERSIONS_HTML --no-check-certificate "https://developer.android.com/about/versions" 2>&1 || exit 1;
LATEST_BETA=$(grep -B4 -A2 'data-icon=\"preview' PIXEL_VERSIONS_HTML | grep -o 'href="/about/versions/.*[0-9]"' | cut -d\" -f2);
[ "$LATEST_BETA" ] || LATEST_BETA=$(grep -oE 'href="/about/versions/[0-9]{2}"' PIXEL_VERSIONS_HTML | cut -d\" -f2 | sort -ru | head -n1);
wget -q -T 10 -O PIXEL_LATEST_HTML --no-check-certificate "https://developer.android.com$LATEST_BETA" 2>&1 || exit 1;
wget -q -T 10 -O PIXEL_FI_HTML --no-check-certificate "https://developer.android.com$(grep -o 'href=".*download.*"' PIXEL_LATEST_HTML | grep -v 'ota' | cut -d\" -f2 | sort -ru | head -n1)" 2>&1 || exit 1;
wget -q -T 10 -O PIXEL_OTA_HTML --no-check-certificate "https://developer.android.com$(grep -o 'href=".*download-ota.*"' PIXEL_LATEST_HTML | cut -d\" -f2 | sort -ru | head -n1)" 2>&1 || exit 1;
SRC=FI; [ "$(grep 'tr id=' PIXEL_FI_HTML | sed 's;.*<tr id="\(.*\)">.*;\1;' | wc -w)" -lt "$(grep 'tr id=' PIXEL_OTA_HTML | sed 's;.*<tr id="\(.*\)">.*;\1;' | wc -w)" ] && SRC=OTA;
MODEL_LIST="$(grep -A1 'tr id=' PIXEL_${SRC}_HTML | grep 'td' | sed 's;.*<td>\(.*\)</td>.*;\1;')";
PRODUCT_LIST="$(grep 'tr id=' PIXEL_${SRC}_HTML | sed 's;.*<tr id="\(.*\)">.*;\1_beta;')";
echo "$PRODUCT_LIST" | wc -w;

if [ "$FORCE_MATCH" ]; then
  DEVICE="$(getprop ro.product.device)";
  case "$(echo ' '$PRODUCT_LIST' ')" in
    *" ${DEVICE}_beta "*)
      MODEL="$(getprop ro.product.model)";
      PRODUCT="${DEVICE}_beta";
    ;;
  esac;
fi;
item "Selecting Pixel Beta device ...";
if [ -z "$PRODUCT" ]; then
  set_random_beta() {
    local list_count="$(echo "$MODEL_LIST" | wc -l)";
    local list_rand="$((RANDOM % $list_count + 1))";
    local IFS=$'\n';
    set -- $MODEL_LIST;
    MODEL="$(eval echo \${$list_rand})";
    set -- $PRODUCT_LIST;
    PRODUCT="$(eval echo \${$list_rand})";
    DEVICE="$(echo "$PRODUCT" | sed 's/_beta//')";
  }
  set_random_beta;
fi;
echo "$MODEL ($PRODUCT)";

item "Crawling Android Flash Tool for latest Pixel Canary build info ...";
wget -q -T 10 -O PIXEL_FLASH_HTML --no-check-certificate "https://flash.android.com/" 2>&1 || exit 1;
wget -q -T 10 -O PIXEL_STATION_JSON --header "Referer: https://flash.android.com" --no-check-certificate "https://content-flashstation-pa.googleapis.com/v1/builds?product=$PRODUCT&key=$(grep -o '<body data-client-config=.*' PIXEL_FLASH_HTML | cut -d\; -f2 | cut -d\& -f1)" 2>&1 || exit 1;
tac PIXEL_STATION_JSON | grep -m1 -A13 '"canary": true' > PIXEL_CANARY_JSON;
ID="$(grep 'releaseCandidateName' PIXEL_CANARY_JSON | cut -d\" -f4)";
INCREMENTAL="$(grep 'buildId' PIXEL_CANARY_JSON | cut -d\" -f4)";
[ -z "$ID" -o -z "$INCREMENTAL" ] && die "Failed to extract build info from JSON";
echo "Android $(grep 'releaseTrackVersionName' PIXEL_CANARY_JSON | cut -d\" -f4)";

FI="$(grep 'factoryImageDownloadUrl' PIXEL_CANARY_JSON | cut -d\" -f4)";
FI_HOST="$(echo "$FI" | sed 's;^.*://\(.*\)$;\1;' | cut -d/ -f1)";
FI_PATH="/$(echo "$FI" | sed 's;^.*://\(.*\)$;\1;' | cut -d/ -f2-)";
if [ "$FI" -a "$FI_HOST" -a "$FI_PATH" ]; then
  nc $FI_HOST 80 <<EOF | tr -d '\r' > PIXEL_ZIP_HEADERS;
HEAD $FI_PATH HTTP/1.1
Host: $FI_HOST
Connection: close

EOF
else
  warn "Failed to extract Factory Image URL from JSON";
fi;
if [ ! -s PIXEL_ZIP_HEADERS ] || ! grep -q 'Last-Modified' PIXEL_ZIP_HEADERS; then
  wget -q -T 10 -S --spider -o PIXEL_ZIP_HEADERS --no-check-certificate "$FI" 2>&1;
fi;
if [ -f PIXEL_ZIP_HEADERS ] && grep -q 'Last-Modified' PIXEL_ZIP_HEADERS; then
  CANARY_REL_DATE="$(date -D '%a, %d %b %Y %H:%M:%S %Z' -d "$(grep -o 'Last-Modified.*' PIXEL_ZIP_HEADERS | cut -d\  -f2-)" '+%Y-%m-%d')";
  CANARY_EXP_DATE="$(date -D '%s' -d "$(($(date -D '%Y-%m-%d' -d "$CANARY_REL_DATE" '+%s') + 60 * 60 * 24 * 7 * 6))" '+%Y-%m-%d')";
  echo "Canary Released: $CANARY_REL_DATE \
    \nEstimated Expiry: $CANARY_EXP_DATE";
else
  warn "Failed to determine Release Date from HTTP headers";
  CANARY_REL_DATE="Unknown";
  CANARY_EXP_DATE="Unknown";
fi;

item "Crawling Pixel Update Bulletins for corresponding security patch level ...";
CANARY_ID="$(grep '"id"' PIXEL_CANARY_JSON | sed -e 's;.*canary-\(.*\)".*;\1;' -e 's;^\(.\{4\}\);\1-;')";
[ -z "$CANARY_ID" ] && die "Failed to extract build info from JSON";
wget -q -T 10 -O PIXEL_SECBULL_HTML --no-check-certificate "https://source.android.com/docs/security/bulletin/pixel" 2>&1 || exit 1;
SECURITY_PATCH="$(grep "<td>$CANARY_ID" PIXEL_SECBULL_HTML | sed 's;.*<td>\(.*\)</td>;\1;')";
if [ -z "$SECURITY_PATCH" ]; then
  warn "Failed to determine exact security patch level from Pixel Update Bulletins";
  item "Assuming probable security patch level from Canary build info ...";
  SECURITY_PATCH="${CANARY_ID}-05";
fi;
echo "$SECURITY_PATCH";

item "Dumping values to minimal pif.prop ...";
cat <<EOF | tee pif.prop;
MANUFACTURER=Google
MODEL=$MODEL
FINGERPRINT=google/$PRODUCT/$DEVICE:CANARY/$ID/$INCREMENTAL:user/release-keys
PRODUCT=$PRODUCT
DEVICE=$DEVICE
SECURITY_PATCH=$SECURITY_PATCH
DEVICE_INITIAL_SDK_INT=32
EOF

for MIGRATE in migrate.sh /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/migrate.sh; do
  [ -f "$MIGRATE" ] && break; 
done;
if [ -f "$MIGRATE" ]; then
  for OLDPIF in /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/custom.pif.prop /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/custom.pif.json; do
    [ -f "$OLDPIF" ] && break;
  done;
  if [ -f "$OLDPIF" ]; then
    grep -q '//"\*.security_patch"' $OLDPIF && PATCH_COMMENT=1;
    grep -q '#\*.security_patch' $OLDPIF && PATCH_COMMENT=1;
    grep -qE "verboseLogs|VERBOSE_LOGS" $OLDPIF && ARGS="-a";
  else
    FORCE_STRONG=1;
  fi;
  if [ "$FORCE_STRONG" ]; then
    item "Forcing configuration for <A13 PI Strong ...";
    ARGS="-a"; PATCH_COMMENT=1; spoofProvider=0;
  else
    item "Retaining existing configuration ...";
  fi;
  [ -d /data/adb/tricky_store ] && unset PATCH_COMMENT;
  item "Converting pif.prop to custom.pif.prop with migrate.sh:";
  rm -f pif.json custom.pif.json custom.pif.prop;
  sh $MIGRATE -i $ARGS pif.prop;
  if [ -n "$ARGS" ]; then
    grep_config() {
      if [ -f "$2" ]; then
        case $2 in
          *.json) grep -m1 "$1" $2 | cut -d\" -f4;;
          *.prop) grep -m1 "$1=" "$2" | cut -d= -f2 | cut -d\# -f1 | sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//';;
        esac;
      fi;
    }
    verboseLogs=$(grep_config "VERBOSE_LOGS" $OLDPIF);
    ADVSETTINGS="spoofBuild spoofProps spoofProvider spoofSignature spoofVendingFinger spoofVendingSdk verboseLogs";
    for SETTING in $ADVSETTINGS; do
      eval [ -z \"\$$SETTING\" ] \&\& $SETTING=$(grep_config "$SETTING" $OLDPIF);
      eval TMPVAL=\$$SETTING;
      [ -n "$TMPVAL" ] && sed -i "s;\($SETTING=\).;\1$TMPVAL;" custom.pif.prop;
    done;
  fi;
  [ "$PATCH_COMMENT" ] && sed -i 's;\*.security_patch;#\*.security_patch;' custom.pif.prop;
  echo "\n# Canary Released: $CANARY_REL_DATE\n# Estimated Expiry: $CANARY_EXP_DATE" >> custom.pif.prop;
  cat custom.pif.prop;
fi;

if [ "$DIR" = /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/autopif4 ]; then
  if [ -f /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/migrate.sh ]; then
    NEWNAME="custom.pif.prop";
  else
    NEWNAME="pif.prop";
  fi;
  for OLDPIF in $NEWNAME custom.pif.json; do
    if [ -f "../$OLDPIF" ]; then
      item "Renaming old file to $OLDPIF.bak ...";
      mv -fv ../$OLDPIF ../$OLDPIF.bak;
    fi;
  done;
  item "Installing new prop ...";
  cp -fv $NEWNAME ..;
  TS_DIR=/data/adb/tricky_store;
  if [ -d /data/adb/teesim -o -d /data/adb/omk ]; then
    warn "TEESimulator v4.x/Oh My KeyMint must be configured manually, ensure patch levels match *.security_patch";
  elif [ -d "$TS_DIR" ]; then
    TS_SECPAT=$TS_DIR/security_patch.txt;
    touch $TS_SECPAT;
    if [ -f /data/adb/modules/tricky_store/libTEESimulator.so -o -f /data/adb/modules/tricky_store/libTrickyStoreOSS.so ]; then
      item "Updating TEESimulator/Tricky Store OSS security_patch.txt ...";
      if [ ! -s "$TS_SECPAT" ]; then
        cat <<EOF > $TS_SECPAT;
all=

[com.google.android.gms]
system=no
EOF
      fi;
    else
      item "Updating Tricky Store security_patch.txt ...";
      [ -s "$TS_SECPAT" ] || echo "all=" > $TS_SECPAT;
      grep -qE '^[0-9]{8}$' $TS_SECPAT && sed -i "s/^.*$/${SECURITY_PATCH//-}/" $TS_SECPAT;
      grep -qE '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}$' $TS_SECPAT && sed -i "s/^.*$/$SECURITY_PATCH/" $TS_SECPAT;
    fi;
    if ! grep -q 'all=device_default' $TS_SECPAT; then
      grep -q 'all=' $TS_SECPAT && sed -i "s/all=.*/all=$SECURITY_PATCH/" $TS_SECPAT;
    fi;
    if ! grep -q 'system=no' $TS_SECPAT; then
      grep -q 'system=' $TS_SECPAT && sed -i "s/system=.*/system=$(echo ${SECURITY_PATCH//-} | cut -c-6)/" $TS_SECPAT;
    fi;
    sed -i '$a\' $TS_SECPAT;
    cat $TS_SECPAT;
  fi;
  if [ -f /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/killpi.sh ]; then
    item "Killing any running GMS DroidGuard/Play Store processes ...";
    sh /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/killpi.sh 2>&1 || true;
  fi;
fi;
